Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the complex_analysis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 945. The moduli space has dimension at most 678 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_945(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 945. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^678(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Statement: In the complex_analysis framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 928. The resulting operator achieves bound O(388/n^3).

Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
